# Model List

This file lists all `1193` Stan model directories currently present under `models/`. It is generated from each model's `metadata.json`, the matching SafeStan `record.json` and `SAFETY_NOTE.md`, the latest `cmdsafestan` smoke reports, and the small Stan-vs-SafeStan comparison artifacts already in the repository.

The `stan_case_studies` and `stan_tutorials` rows are retained here for inventory completeness, but the `Duplicate of` column marks the 30 rows that are source-identical copies of `example_models` rows. Those duplicate rows are excluded from compatibility totals in `RESULTS.md`.

## Summary

| Source family | Models |
|---|---:|
| `bda_r_demos` | 14 |
| `betanalpha_knitr_case_studies` | 302 |
| `example_models` | 538 |
| `forestdb` | 101 |
| `posteriordb` | 147 |
| `stan_case_studies` | 15 |
| `stan_tutorials` | 15 |
| `stancon_talks` | 61 |
| **Total** | **1193** |

| Compatibility result | Models |
|---|---:|
| `as-is` | 320 |
| `minor` | 412 |
| `major` | 461 |

- SafeStan translations exist for `732` raw as-is/minor rows: `669` API-generated translations and `63` manually translated/repaired rows. `177` of those have no protected data interface.
- Manual repair smoke check: `54 / 61` targeted compile-failure translations now run under `cmdsafestan`; the remaining `7` are row-wise protected categorical/multinomial cases that still hit SafeStan protected-data checks.
- `data_original.json` is present for `639` rows; all checked-in runnable `data.json` files are marked by metadata as `synthetic_generated` from `synthetic/local`.
- `Result same?` is populated for the `694`-row wrapper comparison in `comparison_runs/full-wrapper-200x1-manual-repaired-20260430/`: `581` same, `109` same-density/MCMC-different, `4` different, and `0` run-failed. `not checked` means no repository comparison artifact exists for that row.
